{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01ML1tyYRpSjedntF2PPqnsk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01SgJRA9DgNAeshWGN8DyHN7","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/qutip-fundamentals.md","content":"# QuTiP Fundamentals for Open Quantum Systems\n\n## Overview\nQuTiP (Quantum Toolbox in Python) is a powerful library for simulating quantum mechanics, open quantum systems, and cavity QED systems. It provides tools for constructing Hamiltonians, Liouvillians, and solving master equations.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install qutip numpy scipy matplotlib\n```\n\n## Key Concepts for Dicke Model Simulations\n\n### 1. Creating Quantum Operators\n\n#### Two-Level System Operators (Spins)\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\n\n# Pauli matrices for a single spin\nsx = qt.sigmax()  # sigma_x\nsy = qt.sigmay()  # sigma_y\nsz = qt.sigmaz()  # sigma_z\n\n# Ladder operators\nsp = qt.sigmap()  # sigma_+  (raising operator)\nsm = qt.sigmam()  # sigma_-  (lowering operator)\n\n# Identity\nI = qt.qeye(2)    # 2x2 identity matrix\n```\n\n#### Cavity Mode Operators\n```python\n# Cavity operators (harmonic oscillator)\n# For dimension n_max (photon number cutoff)\nn_max = 16\na = qt.destroy(n_max)      # annihilation operator\nad = qt.create(n_max)       # creation operator: a_dagger\nn = qt.num(n_max)           # number operator: a_dagger * a\n```\n\n#### Many-Body Collective Operators\n```python\n# For N identical two-level systems\nN = 4\n# Collective angular momentum operators\nJx = 0.5 * sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, qt.sigmax(), qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1))\n                  for i in range(N)])\nJy = 0.5 * sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, qt.sigmay(), qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1))\n                  for i in range(N)])\nJz = 0.5 * sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, qt.sigmaz(), qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1))\n                  for i in range(N)])\nJp = Jx + 1j*Jy  # J_+\nJm = Jx - 1j*Jy  # J_-\n```\n\n### 2. Constructing Composite Hilbert Spaces\n\n```python\n# Create tensor product space: cavity + spins\nN_spins = 4\nn_max = 16\ncavity = qt.destroy(n_max)\nidentity_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\nidentity_spin = qt.qeye(2**N_spins)\n\n# Operators on composite space\na = qt.tensor(cavity, identity_spin)\nad = qt.tensor(cavity.dag(), identity_spin)\n\n# Collective spin operators on composite space\n# (constructed as shown above on spin space, then tensored with cavity identity)\n```\n\n### 3. Building the Hamiltonian\n\n```python\n# Dicke model Hamiltonian\nomega0 = 1.0      # Rabi frequency for spins\nomegac = 1.0      # Cavity frequency\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)  # Coupling constant\n\n# H = omega0 * Jz + omegac * a_dagger * a + g * (a_dagger + a) * (J+ + J-)\nH = omega0 * Jz + omegac * a.dag()*a + g * (a.dag() + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n```\n\n### 4. Creating Jump Operators and Liouvillian\n\nJump operators describe dissipation channels:\n\n```python\n# Cavity decay (photon loss)\nkappa = 1.0\nc_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n# Single-qubit dephasing\ngamma_phi = 0.01\nfor i in range(N):\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_i)  # dephasing on qubit i\n\n# Single-qubit decay (spontaneous emission)\ngamma_down = 0.1\nfor i in range(N):\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * sigma_minus_i)\n\n# Collective excitation operators\ngamma_up = 0.1\nc_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * Jp)  # collective pumping\n\ngamma_collective_down = 0.1\nc_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_collective_down) * Jm)  # collective emission\n\n# Create Liouvillian (master equation operator)\n# Liouville operator: L[rho] = -i[H, rho] + sum_k (c_k * rho * c_k_dagger - 0.5*{c_k_dagger*c_k, rho})\nliouvillian = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n```\n\n### 5. Solving Steady State\n\n```python\n# Solve steady state: L[rho_ss] = 0\n# Using direct solver (good for smaller systems)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\n\n# rho_ss is the steady state density matrix\n```\n\n### 6. Partial Trace (Trace Out Spins)\n\n```python\n# If rho_ss is in composite space (cavity + spins)\n# Trace out the spins to get cavity state\nN_spins = 4\n# Spins are in subsystem 1, cavity in subsystem 0\n# For composite system, subsystem indices follow tensor product order\n\ncavity_state = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)  # Extract cavity reduced density matrix\n```\n\n### 7. Calculating Wigner Function\n\n```python\n# Wigner function for a cavity mode state\nxvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)  # position\npvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)  # momentum\n\n# Calculate Wigner function\nW = qt.wigner(cavity_state, xvec, pvec)\n\n# W is a 2D array: W[i, j] corresponds to Wigner function at (xvec[i], pvec[j])\n```\n\n## Tips for Dicke Model Implementation\n\n1. **Hilbert Space Dimension**: For N qubits + cavity with n_max photons, total dimension is 2^N * n_max\n2. **Steady State Solver**: For large systems, use `steadystate()` method which has efficient algorithms\n3. **Memory Efficiency**: For N=4, 2^4 * 16 = 256-dimensional space is manageable\n4. **Trace Operations**: Use `ptrace()` with correct indices to extract subsystem states\n5. **Wigner Function**: Quadratures x,p are related to cavity quadrature operators\n\n## References\n- QuTiP Documentation: https://qutip.org/\n- Dicke Model Papers: https://arxiv.org/pdf/1608.06293\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":126,"cache_read_input_tokens":19183,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":126,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a4cfc222-781e-4c6b-b5e3-8e29761b7432","uuid":"a6975dca-fb58-4ea5-b9e3-47e25444748a"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_01V8ntUeQUVeBEjs32L5ebiv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01RV4VYxAP8A98T4eyGQGicc","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/quantum-operators-construction.md","content":"# Constructing Quantum Operators for Many-Body Systems\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers advanced techniques for constructing operators on composite Hilbert spaces with multiple subsystems (cavity + spins in our case).\n\n## Single-Subsystem Operators\n\n### Cavity Operators\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\nn_max = 16\n\n# Lowering and raising operators\na = qt.destroy(n_max)      # annihilation: |n> -> |n-1>\nad = qt.create(n_max)      # creation: |n> -> |n+1>\nn = a.dag() * a            # number operator\nx = (a + ad) / np.sqrt(2)  # position quadrature\np = 1j * (ad - a) / np.sqrt(2)  # momentum quadrature\n```\n\n### Single Qubit Operators\n```python\n# Pauli operators\nI = qt.qeye(2)\nsx = qt.sigmax()\nsy = qt.sigmay()\nsz = qt.sigmaz()\n\n# Ladder operators\nsp = qt.sigmap()   # |0><1|\nsm = qt.sigmam()   # |1><0|\n\n# Projection operators\nP0 = (I + sz) / 2  # |0><0|\nP1 = (I - sz) / 2  # |1><1|\n```\n\n## Building Many-Body Operators (N-Qubit Systems)\n\n### Method 1: Direct Summation (For Collective Operators)\n\n```python\ndef build_collective_operator(N, single_op, position=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build an operator acting on a specific qubit in a chain of N identical qubits.\n\n    Args:\n        N: number of qubits\n        single_op: operator acting on the target qubit (2x2 matrix)\n        position: which qubit to act on (0 to N-1)\n\n    Returns:\n        Operator on 2^N dimensional Hilbert space\n    \"\"\"\n    ops = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    ops[position] = single_op\n    return qt.tensor(*ops)\n\n# Example: sigma_z on qubit 0\nsigma_z_0 = build_collective_operator(4, qt.sigmaz(), 0)\n\n# Sum of all single-qubit operators\nJz = sum([build_collective_operator(4, 0.5*qt.sigmaz(), i) for i in range(4)])\nJp = sum([build_collective_operator(4, 0.5*qt.sigmap(), i) for i in range(4)])\nJm = sum([build_collective_operator(4, 0.5*qt.sigmam(), i) for i in range(4)])\n```\n\n### Method 2: Using Efficient Tensor Construction\n\n```python\ndef build_collective_z(N):\n    \"\"\"Efficient construction of J_z = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_z^(i)\"\"\"\n    result = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmaz()\n        term = qt.tensor(*ops)\n        if result is None:\n            result = term\n        else:\n            result += term\n    return result\n\ndef build_collective_pm(N):\n    \"\"\"Efficient construction of J_+/- = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_+/-^(i)\"\"\"\n    jp = None\n    jm = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        ops_p = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops_p[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmap()\n        ops_m = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops_m[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmam()\n\n        jp = qt.tensor(*ops_p) if jp is None else jp + qt.tensor(*ops_p)\n        jm = qt.tensor(*ops_m) if jm is None else jm + qt.tensor(*ops_m)\n\n    return jp, jm\n```\n\n## Composite Hilbert Space (Cavity + Spins)\n\n### Creating Operators on Composite Space\n\n```python\nN = 4           # number of spins\nn_max = 16      # photon number cutoff\n\n# Build on separate spaces first\n# Cavity operators (6-dim for n_max=16 actually, but we use 16 for flexibility)\na_cavity = qt.destroy(n_max)\nad_cavity = qt.create(n_max)\n\n# Spin operators\nJz_spins = build_collective_z(N)\nJp_spins, Jm_spins = build_collective_pm(N)\n\n# Identity operators\nI_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\nI_spins = qt.qeye(2**N)\n\n# Extend to composite space: cavity is first subsystem, spins second\na = qt.tensor(a_cavity, I_spins)\nad = qt.tensor(ad_cavity, I_spins)\nJz = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jz_spins)\nJp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jp_spins)\nJm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jm_spins)\n```\n\n## Dissipation Operators (Lindblad Jump Operators)\n\n### Cavity Dissipation\n```python\ndef cavity_decay_ops(N, n_max, kappa):\n    \"\"\"Cavity photon loss\"\"\"\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    I_spins = qt.qeye(2**N)\n    cavity_decay = qt.tensor(a, I_spins)\n    return [np.sqrt(kappa) * cavity_decay]\n```\n\n### Single-Qubit Dephasing\n```python\ndef dephasing_ops(N, n_max, gamma_phi):\n    \"\"\"Local dephasing on each qubit\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    ops = []\n\n    for i in range(N):\n        # sigma_z on qubit i\n        sigma_z_i = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        sigma_z_i[i] = qt.sigmaz()\n        sz = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*sigma_z_i))\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * sz)\n\n    return ops\n```\n\n### Single-Qubit Decay/Excitation\n```python\ndef single_qubit_decay_ops(N, n_max, gamma_down):\n    \"\"\"Local spontaneous emission (decay)\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    ops = []\n\n    for i in range(N):\n        # sigma_- on qubit i\n        sigma_m_i = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        sigma_m_i[i] = qt.sigmam()\n        sm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*sigma_m_i))\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * sm)\n\n    return ops\n\ndef single_qubit_pump_ops(N, n_max, gamma_up):\n    \"\"\"Local excitation (pumping)\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    ops = []\n\n    for i in range(N):\n        # sigma_+ on qubit i\n        sigma_p_i = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        sigma_p_i[i] = qt.sigmap()\n        sp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*sigma_p_i))\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * sp)\n\n    return ops\n```\n\n### Collective Decay/Excitation\n```python\ndef collective_decay_ops(N, n_max, gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective emission: J_- = sum_i sigma_-^(i) / 2\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    Jm_spins = build_collective_z(N)  # Use appropriate collective operator\n    Jm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jm_spins)\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jm]\n\ndef collective_pump_ops(N, n_max, gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective excitation: J_+ = sum_i sigma_+^(i) / 2\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    Jp_spins, _ = build_collective_pm(N)\n    Jp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jp_spins)\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jp]\n```\n\n## Key Patterns for Dicke Model\n\n### Structure of Operators\n- Cavity subsystem: dimension n_max\n- Spin subsystem: dimension 2^N\n- Composite: dimension n_max * 2^N\n- Tensor product ordering: (cavity, spins)\n\n### Efficiency Considerations\n1. **Operator Construction**: Build on separate spaces, then tensor\n2. **Memory**: For N=4, dimension is 16*16=256, manageable\n3. **Steady State**: QuTiP's `steadystate()` handles composite spaces efficiently\n4. **Partial Trace**: Use `qt.ptrace(rho, 0)` to extract cavity state\n\n## Reference Code Structure\n\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Parameters\nN = 4\nn_max = 16\nomega0 = omega_c = 1.0\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)\nkappa = 1.0\ngamma_phi = 0.01\n\n# Build operators on composite space\na = qt.tensor(qt.destroy(n_max), qt.qeye(2**N))\nJz = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), build_collective_z(N))\n\n# Hamiltonian\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * a.dag()*a + g * Constructing Quantum Operators for Many-Body Systems\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers advanced techniques for constructing operators on composite Hilbert spaces with multiple subsystems (cavity + spins in our case).\n\n## Single-Subsystem Operators\n\n### Cavity Operators\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\nn_max = 16\n\n# Lowering and raising operators\na = qt.destroy(n_max)      # annihilation: |n> -> |n-1>\nad = qt.create(n_max)      # creation: |n> -> |n+1>\nn = a.dag() * a            # number operator\nx = (a + ad) / np.sqrt(2)  # position quadrature\np = 1j * (ad - a) / np.sqrt(2)  # momentum quadrature\n```\n\n### Single Qubit Operators\n```python\n# Pauli operators\nI = qt.qeye(2)\nsx = qt.sigmax()\nsy = qt.sigmay()\nsz = qt.sigmaz()\n\n# Ladder operators\nsp = qt.sigmap()   # |0><1|\nsm = qt.sigmam()   # |1><0|\n\n# Projection operators\nP0 = (I + sz) / 2  # |0><0|\nP1 = (I - sz) / 2  # |1><1|\n```\n\n## Building Many-Body Operators (N-Qubit Systems)\n\n### Method 1: Direct Summation (For Collective Operators)\n\n```python\ndef build_collective_operator(N, single_op, position=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build an operator acting on a specific qubit in a chain of N identical qubits.\n\n    Args:\n        N: number of qubits\n        single_op: operator acting on the target qubit (2x2 matrix)\n        position: which qubit to act on (0 to N-1)\n\n    Returns:\n        Operator on 2^N dimensional Hilbert space\n    \"\"\"\n    ops = [qt.qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n    ops[position] = single_op\n    return qt.tensor(*ops)\n\n# Example: sigma_z on qubit 0\nsigma_z_0 = build_collective_operator(4, qt.sigmaz(), 0)\n\n# Sum of all single-qubit operators\nJz = sum([build_collective_operator(4, 0.5*qt.sigmaz(), i) for i in range(4)])\nJp = sum([build_collective_operator(4, 0.5*qt.sigmap(), i) for i in range(4)])\nJm = sum([build_collective_operator(4, 0.5*qt.sigmam(), i) for i in range(4)])\n```\n\n### Method 2: Using Efficient Tensor Construction\n\n```python\ndef build_collective_z(N):\n    \"\"\"Efficient construction of J_z = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_z^(i)\"\"\"\n    result = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmaz()\n        term = qt.tensor(*ops)\n        if result is None:\n            result = term\n        else:\n            result += term\n    return result\n\ndef build_collective_pm(N):\n    \"\"\"Efficient construction of J_+/- = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_+/-^(i)\"\"\"\n    jp = None\n    jm = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        ops_p = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops_p[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmap()\n        ops_m = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops_m[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmam()\n\n        jp = qt.tensor(*ops_p) if jp is None else jp + qt.tensor(*ops_p)\n        jm = qt.tensor(*ops_m) if jm is None else jm + qt.tensor(*ops_m)\n\n    return jp, jm\n```\n\n## Composite Hilbert Space (Cavity + Spins)\n\n### Creating Operators on Composite Space\n\n```python\nN = 4           # number of spins\nn_max = 16      # photon number cutoff\n\n# Build on separate spaces first\n# Cavity operators (6-dim for n_max=16 actually, but we use 16 for flexibility)\na_cavity = qt.destroy(n_max)\nad_cavity = qt.create(n_max)\n\n# Spin operators\nJz_spins = build_collective_z(N)\nJp_spins, Jm_spins = build_collective_pm(N)\n\n# Identity operators\nI_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\nI_spins = qt.qeye(2**N)\n\n# Extend to composite space: cavity is first subsystem, spins second\na = qt.tensor(a_cavity, I_spins)\nad = qt.tensor(ad_cavity, I_spins)\nJz = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jz_spins)\nJp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jp_spins)\nJm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jm_spins)\n```\n\n## Dissipation Operators (Lindblad Jump Operators)\n\n### Cavity Dissipation\n```python\ndef cavity_decay_ops(N, n_max, kappa):\n    \"\"\"Cavity photon loss\"\"\"\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    I_spins = qt.qeye(2**N)\n    cavity_decay = qt.tensor(a, I_spins)\n    return [np.sqrt(kappa) * cavity_decay]\n```\n\n### Single-Qubit Dephasing\n```python\ndef dephasing_ops(N, n_max, gamma_phi):\n    \"\"\"Local dephasing on each qubit\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    ops = []\n\n    for i in range(N):\n        # sigma_z on qubit i\n        sigma_z_i = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        sigma_z_i[i] = qt.sigmaz()\n        sz = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*sigma_z_i))\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * sz)\n\n    return ops\n```\n\n### Single-Qubit Decay/Excitation\n```python\ndef single_qubit_decay_ops(N, n_max, gamma_down):\n    \"\"\"Local spontaneous emission (decay)\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    ops = []\n\n    for i in range(N):\n        # sigma_- on qubit i\n        sigma_m_i = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        sigma_m_i[i] = qt.sigmam()\n        sm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*sigma_m_i))\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * sm)\n\n    return ops\n\ndef single_qubit_pump_ops(N, n_max, gamma_up):\n    \"\"\"Local excitation (pumping)\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    ops = []\n\n    for i in range(N):\n        # sigma_+ on qubit i\n        sigma_p_i = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        sigma_p_i[i] = qt.sigmap()\n        sp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*sigma_p_i))\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * sp)\n\n    return ops\n```\n\n### Collective Decay/Excitation\n```python\ndef collective_decay_ops(N, n_max, gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective emission: J_- = sum_i sigma_-^(i) / 2\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    Jm_spins = build_collective_z(N)  # Use appropriate collective operator\n    Jm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jm_spins)\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jm]\n\ndef collective_pump_ops(N, n_max, gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective excitation: J_+ = sum_i sigma_+^(i) / 2\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    Jp_spins, _ = build_collective_pm(N)\n    Jp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jp_spins)\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jp]\n```\n\n## Key Patterns for Dicke Model\n\n### Structure of Operators\n- Cavity subsystem: dimension n_max\n- Spin subsystem: dimension 2^N\n- Composite: dimension n_max * 2^N\n- Tensor product ordering: (cavity, spins)\n\n### Efficiency Considerations\n1. **Operator Construction**: Build on separate spaces, then tensor\n2. **Memory**: For N=4, dimension is 16*16=256, manageable\n3. **Steady State**: QuTiP's `steadystate()` handles composite spaces efficiently\n4. **Partial Trace**: Use `qt.ptrace(rho, 0)` to extract cavity state\n\n## Reference Code Structure\n\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Parameters\nN = 4\nn_max = 16\nomega0 = omega_c = 1.0\ng = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)\nkappa = 1.0\ngamma_phi = 0.01\n\n# Build operators on composite space\na = qt.tensor(qt.destroy(n_max), qt.qeye(2**N))\nJz = qt.tensor(qt.qeye(n_max), build_collective_z(N))\n\n# Hamiltonian\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * a.dag()*a + g * (a.dag() + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\n# Jump operators\nc_ops = cavity_decay_ops(N, n_max, kappa)\nc_ops.extend(dephasing_ops(N, n_max, gamma_phi))\n\n# Steady state\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\n\n# Extract cavity state\nrho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n\n# Wigner function\nW = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, np.linspace(-6,6,1000), np.linspace(-6,6,1000))\n```\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n1. **Tensor Product Order**: Always consistent (cavity first, then spins)\n2. **Normalization**: Jump operators include sqrt(rate), not just rate\n3. **Qubit Indexing**: 0-indexed from left to right\n4. **Dimensions**: Check that all operators match composite space dimension\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

For cavity modes, it provides insight into quantum properties like non-classicality and entanglement dynamics.\n\n## Theoretical Background\n\n### Definition\nFor a quantum state ρ, the Wigner function W(x,p) is:\n\n$$W(x,p) = \\frac{1}{\\pi} \\text{Tr}\\left[\\rho \\, D(z)\\right]$$\n\nwhere D(z) is the displacement operator with z = (x + ip)/√2.\n\n### Interpretation\n- **Gaussian states** (coherent, thermal): positive Wigner function\n- **Non-classical states** (Fock, squeezed): negative regions (non-positive)\n- **Purity indicator**: Integrated area and symmetries reflect quantum character\n\n## QuTiP Implementation\n\n### Basic Wigner Function Calculation\n\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n# Define evaluation grid\nxvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\npvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n\n# Cavity state (density matrix from steady state)\nrho_cavity = cavity_state  # from qt.ptrace()\n\n# Calculate Wigner function\nW = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n# W is shape (len(xvec), len(pvec))\n# W[i, j] corresponds to Wigner function value at (xvec[i], pvec[j])\n```\n\n### Understanding the Output Format\n\n```python\n# Shape: (n_points_x, n_points_y)\nn_x = len(xvec)  # 1000\nn_y = len(pvec)  # 1000\n\nW_shape = W.shape  # (1000, 1000)\n\n# Access value at specific point\nx_idx, p_idx = 500, 500  # center of grid\nW_center = W[x_idx, p_idx]  # Wigner function at (xvec[500], pvec[500])\n```\n\n## Saving Wigner Function Data\n\n### 1. CSV Format (Flattened Array)\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Calculate Wigner function\nW = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n# Method 1: Save with coordinates (verbose but informative)\ndata_list = []\nfor i, x in enumerate(xvec):\n    for j, p in enumerate(pvec):\n        data_list.append({'x': x, 'p': p, 'W': W[i, j]})\n\ndf = pd.DataFrame(data_list)\ndf.to_csv('wigner_function.csv', index=False)\n\n# Method 2: Save just the 2D array as CSV (more compact)\nnp.savetxt('wigner_function.csv', W, delimiter=',')\n\n# Method 3: Save with metadata in first row\nheader = f\"Grid: x={xvec[0]:.2f} to {xvec[-1]:.2f}, p={pvec[0]:.2f} to {pvec[-1]:.2f}\"\nnp.savetxt('wigner_function.csv', W, delimiter=',', header=header)\n```\n\n### 2. CSV with Grid Information\n\n```python\nimport csv\n\ndef save_wigner_with_grid(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save Wigner function with full coordinate information\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'w', newline='') as f:\n        writer = csv.writer(f)\n\n        # Write header with x coordinates\n        writer.writerow(['p\\\\x'] + [f'{x:.6f}' for x in xvec])\n\n        # Write rows with p coordinate and Wigner values\n        for j, p in enumerate(pvec):\n            writer.writerow([f'{p:.6f}'] + [f'{W[i,j]:.10e}' for i in range(len(xvec))])\n\nsave_wigner_with_grid(W, xvec, pvec, 'wigner_function.csv')\n```\n\n## Batch Processing Multiple Cases\n\n### Processing Dictionary of Cases\n\n```python\ndef process_and_save_all_cases(cases_dict, output_dir='./'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process multiple Dicke model cases and save results\n\n    Args:\n        cases_dict: dict with case names and parameters\n        output_dir: directory to save CSV files\n    \"\"\"\n    import os\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for case_name, case_params in cases_dict.items():\n        # Solve steady state for this case\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(case_params['H'], case_params['c_ops'])\n\n        # Extract cavity state\n        rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n\n        # Calculate Wigner function\n        xvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n        pvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n        W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n        # Save to CSV\n        filename = f\"{output_dir}/{case_name}.csv\"\n        np.savetxt(filename, W, delimiter=',')\n\n        results[case_name] = {\n            'rho_cavity': rho_cavity,\n            'W': W,\n            'file': filename\n        }\n\n    return results\n```\n\n## Analysis and Validation\n\n### Computing Wigner Function Properties\n\n```python\ndef analyze_wigner(W, xvec, pvec):\n    \"\"\"Compute properties of Wigner function\"\"\"\n    import numpy as np\n\n    dx = xvec[1] - xvec[0] if len(xvec) > 1 else 1\n    dp = pvec[1] - pvec[0] if len(pvec) > 1 else 1\n\n    properties = {\n        'mean_x': np.average(xvec[:, None], weights=W) * dx,\n        'mean_p': np.average(pvec[None, :], weights=W) * dp,\n        'max_value': np.max(W),\n        'min_value': np.min(W),\n        'negative_regions': np.sum(W < 0) * dx * dp,  # presence of non-classicality\n        'total_probability': np.sum(W) * dx * dp,  # should be ~1\n        'is_positive': np.all(W >= -1e-6)  # tolerance for numerical errors\n    }\n\n    return properties\n\n# Example\nprops = analyze_wigner(W, xvec, pvec)\nprint(f\"Minimum Wigner value: {props['min_value']}\")\nprint(f\"Has negative regions: {not props['is_positive']}\")\n```\n\n### Extracting Cavity State Properties\n\n```python\ndef cavity_state_properties(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Extract quantum properties from cavity state\"\"\"\n\n    properties = {\n        'purity': float((rho_cavity * rho_cavity).tr()),\n        'entropy': float(qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)),\n        'photon_number_mean': float(qt.expect(qt.num(rho_cavity.dims[0][0]), rho_cavity)),\n        'photon_number_var': float(qt.expect(qt.num(rho_cavity.dims[0][0])**2, rho_cavity) -\n                                    qt.expect(qt.num(rho_cavity.dims[0][0]), rho_cavity)**2),\n    }\n\n    return properties\n```\n\n## Visualization\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom matplotlib import cm\n\ndef plot_wigner(W, xvec, pvec, title='', cmap='RdBu'):\n    \"\"\"Plot Wigner function\"\"\"\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8))\n\n    # Create mesh grid\n    X, P = np.meshgrid(xvec, pvec, indexing='ij')\n\n    # Plot contours\n    levels = np.linspace(np.min(W), np.max(W), 20)\n    cf = ax.contourf(X, P, W, levels=levels, cmap=cmap)\n    ax.contour(X, P, W, levels=[0], colors='black', linewidths=0.5)\n\n    ax.set_xlabel('x (position)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('p (momentum)')\n    ax.set_title(title)\n\n    cbar = plt.colorbar(cf, ax=ax)\n    cbar.set_label('W(x,p)')\n\n    return fig, ax\n\n# Usage\nfig, ax = plot_wigner(W, xvec, pvec, title='Cavity Wigner Function')\nplt.savefig('wigner_function.png', dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n```\n\n## Performance Notes\n\n### Grid Size Considerations\n- **1000x1000 grid**: High resolution, ~1MB per file in CSV\n- **Computation time**: Usually fast (<1 second) for cavity states\n- **Memory**: Store W matrix in memory before saving\n\n### Numerical Stability\n\n```python\n# QuTiP handles normalization internally\nW = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n# Verify normalization (should integrate to 1)\ndx = xvec[1] - xvec[0]\ndp = pvec[1] - pvec[0]\nintegral = np.sum(W) * dx * dp\nprint(f\"Wigner function integral: {integral:.4f} (should be ~1.0)\")\n```\n\n## Common Issues and Solutions\n\n### Issue: Large Oscillations in Wigner Function\n- **Cause**: Numerical calculation of Wigner function Write header with x coordinates\n        writer.writerow(['p\\\\x'] + [f'{x:.6f}' for x in xvec])\n\n        # Write rows with p coordinate and Wigner values\n        for j, p in enumerate(pvec):\n            writer.writerow([f'{p:.6f}'] + [f'{W[i,j]:.10e}' for i in range(len(xvec))])\n\nsave_wigner_with_grid(W, xvec, pvec, 'wigner_function.csv')\n```\n\n## Batch Processing Multiple Cases\n\n### Processing Dictionary of Cases\n\n```python\ndef process_and_save_all_cases(cases_dict, output_dir='./'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Process multiple Dicke model cases and save results\n\n    Args:\n        cases_dict: dict with case names and parameters\n        output_dir: directory to save CSV files\n    \"\"\"\n    import os\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for case_name, case_params in cases_dict.items():\n        # Solve steady state for this case\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(case_params['H'], case_params['c_ops'])\n\n        # Extract cavity state\n        rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n\n        # Calculate Wigner function\n        xvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n        pvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n        W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n        # Save to CSV\n        filename = f\"{output_dir}/{case_name}.csv\"\n        np.savetxt(filename, W, delimiter=',')\n\n        results[case_name] = {\n            'rho_cavity': rho_cavity,\n            'W': W,\n            'file': filename\n        }\n\n    return results\n```\n\n## Analysis and Validation\n\n### Computing Wigner Function Properties\n\n```python\ndef analyze_wigner(W, xvec, pvec):\n    \"\"\"Compute properties of Wigner function\"\"\"\n    import numpy as np\n\n    dx = xvec[1] - xvec[0] if len(xvec) > 1 else 1\n    dp = pvec[1] - pvec[0] if len(pvec) > 1 else 1\n\n    properties = {\n        'mean_x': np.average(xvec[:, None], weights=W) * dx,\n        'mean_p': np.average(pvec[None, :], weights=W) * dp,\n        'max_value': np.max(W),\n        'min_value': np.min(W),\n        'negative_regions': np.sum(W < 0) * dx * dp,  # presence of non-classicality\n        'total_probability': np.sum(W) * dx * dp,  # should be ~1\n        'is_positive': np.all(W >= -1e-6)  # tolerance for numerical errors\n    }\n\n    return properties\n\n# Example\nprops = analyze_wigner(W, xvec, pvec)\nprint(f\"Minimum Wigner value: {props['min_value']}\")\nprint(f\"Has negative regions: {not props['is_positive']}\")\n```\n\n### Extracting Cavity State Properties\n\n```python\ndef cavity_state_properties(rho_cavity):\n    \"\"\"Extract quantum properties from cavity state\"\"\"\n\n    properties = {\n        'purity': float((rho_cavity * rho_cavity).tr()),\n        'entropy': float(qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)),\n        'photon_number_mean': float(qt.expect(qt.num(rho_cavity.dims[0][0]), rho_cavity)),\n        'photon_number_var': float(qt.expect(qt.num(rho_cavity.dims[0][0])**2, rho_cavity) -\n                                    qt.expect(qt.num(rho_cavity.dims[0][0]), rho_cavity)**2),\n    }\n\n    return properties\n```\n\n## Visualization\n\n```python\nimport matplotlib.pyplot as plt\nfrom matplotlib import cm\n\ndef plot_wigner(W, xvec, pvec, title='', cmap='RdBu'):\n    \"\"\"Plot Wigner function\"\"\"\n    fig, ax = plt.subplots(figsize=(10, 8))\n\n    # Create mesh grid\n    X, P = np.meshgrid(xvec, pvec, indexing='ij')\n\n    # Plot contours\n    levels = np.linspace(np.min(W), np.max(W), 20)\n    cf = ax.contourf(X, P, W, levels=levels, cmap=cmap)\n    ax.contour(X, P, W, levels=[0], colors='black', linewidths=0.5)\n\n    ax.set_xlabel('x (position)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('p (momentum)')\n    ax.set_title(title)\n\n    cbar = plt.colorbar(cf, ax=ax)\n    cbar.set_label('W(x,p)')\n\n    return fig, ax\n\n# Usage\nfig, ax = plot_wigner(W, xvec, pvec, title='Cavity Wigner Function')\nplt.savefig('wigner_function.png', dpi=150, bbox_inches='tight')\n```\n\n## Performance Notes\n\n### Grid Size Considerations\n- **1000x1000 grid**: High resolution, ~1MB per file in CSV\n- **Computation time**: Usually fast (<1 second) for cavity states\n- **Memory**: Store W matrix in memory before saving\n\n### Numerical Stability\n\n```python\n# QuTiP handles normalization internally\nW = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n# Verify normalization (should integrate to 1)\ndx = xvec[1] - xvec[0]\ndp = pvec[1] - pvec[0]\nintegral = np.sum(W) * dx * dp\nprint(f\"Wigner function integral: {integral:.4f} (should be ~1.0)\")\n```\n\n## Common Issues and Solutions\n\n### Issue: Large Oscillations in Wigner Function\n- **Cause**: Numerical calculation of Wigner function on discrete grid\n- **Solution**: Use higher resolution or smoother state representation\n\n### Issue: Wigner Function Negative Values\n- **Cause**: Non-classical quantum states (expected!)\n- **Solution**: This indicates quantum properties; verify with state properties\n\n### Issue: CSV File Too Large\n- **Cause**: 1000x1000 grid * 8 bytes (float64) * multiple cases\n- **Solution**: Consider lower resolution, or compress files\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

The steady state is the time-asymptotic state where the system reaches equilibrium under dissipation. This skill covers solving for steady states efficiently in QuTiP.\n\n## Lindblad Master Equation\n\n### Mathematical Formulation\n\n$$\\frac{d\\rho}{dt} = -\\frac{i}{\\hbar}[H, \\rho] + \\sum_k \\left(L_k \\rho L_k^\\dagger - \\frac{1}{2}\\{L_k^\\dagger L_k, \\rho\\}\\right)$$\n\nWhere:\n- **H**: System Hamiltonian\n- **L_k**: Lindblad (jump) operators describing dissipation channels\n- **{,}**: Anticommutator\n\n### Steady State Condition\nAt steady state: dρ/dt = 0, so L[ρ_ss] = 0\n\n## QuTiP Steady State Solver\n\n### Basic Usage\n\n```python\nimport qutip as qt\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Define system\nH = ...  # Hamiltonian\nc_ops = [...]  # List of jump operators\n\n# Solve steady state\n# Method 1: Direct solver (default, good for small-to-medium systems)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\n\n# Method 2: With options\nopts = qt.Options(nsteps=5000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n```\n\n### Return Value\n- **rho_ss**: Density matrix of shape (N, N) where N is Hilbert space dimension\n- Normalized: Tr(ρ_ss) = 1\n- Hermitian: ρ_ss = ρ_ss†\n\n## Solver Methods and Performance\n\n### Available Solvers\n\n```python\n# Specify solver method\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')  # Direct linear solve\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='iterative')  # Iterative solver\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='svd')  # SVD-based solver\n\n# Method selection based on system size:\n# - 'direct': < 1000 dimensional, dense matrices\n# - 'iterative': > 1000 dimensional, sparse matrices\n# - 'svd': Numerical stability, smaller systems\n```\n\n## Constructing the Liouvillian\n\n### Manual Liouvillian Construction\n\n```python\ndef construct_liouvillian(H, c_ops):\n    \"\"\"\n    Manually construct Liouvillian superoperator\n\n    L[ρ] = -i[H, ρ] + sum_k (L_k ρ L_k† - 1/2 {L_k† L_k, ρ})\n    \"\"\"\n    # QuTiP provides the liouvillian function\n    L = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n    return L\n\n# Check Liouvillian properties\nH = ...\nc_ops = [...]\nL = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n\n# Verify L is superoperator\nprint(f\"Liouvillian shape: {L.shape}\")  # (N^2, N^2)\nprint(f\"Is superoperator: {isinstance(L, qt.Qobj)}\")\n```\n\n### Steady State from Eigenvalue Problem\n\n```python\ndef steady_state_from_eigenvalue(H, c_ops):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve steady state using eigenvalue decomposition\n\n    Steady state is eigenstate with eigenvalue 0 of Liouvillian\n    \"\"\"\n    L = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n\n    # Get eigenvalues and eigenvectors\n    evals, evecs = np.linalg.eig(L.full())\n\n    # Find eigenstate with eigenvalue closest to 0\n    idx = np.argmin(np.abs(evals))\n    rho_vec = evecs[:, idx]\n\n    # Reshape to density matrix\n    N = int(np.sqrt(len(rho_vec)))\n    rho_ss = qt.Qobj(rho_vec.reshape(N, N), dims=[[N], [N]])\n\n    # Normalize\n    rho_ss = rho_ss / rho_ss.tr()\n\n    return rho_ss\n```\n\n## Jump Operators and Dissipation Channels\n\n### Proper Normalization\n\n```python\ndef create_jump_operators(N, n_max, rates):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create normalized jump operators for Dicke model\n\n    Jump operators L_k represent dissipation with rate γ_k\n    Convention: c_ops includes sqrt(γ_k) * L_k\n    \"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    I_spins = qt.qeye(2**N)\n\n    c_ops = []\n\n    # Cavity decay (single decay channel)\n    if 'kappa' in rates:\n        a = qt.tensor(qt.destroy(n_max), I_spins)\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['kappa']) * a)\n\n    # Local dephasing\n    if 'gamma_phi' in rates:\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = qt.sigmaz()\n            sigma_z_i = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_phi']) * sigma_z_i)\n\n    # Local decay/emission\n    if 'gamma_down' in rates:\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = qt.sigmam()\n            sigma_m_i = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_down']) * sigma_m_i)\n\n    # Local excitation/pumping\n    if 'gamma_up' in rates:\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = qt.sigmap()\n            sigma_p_i = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_up']) * sigma_p_i)\n\n    # Collective decay\n    if 'gamma_collective_down' in rates:\n        Jm_spins = sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, 0.5*qt.sigmam(),\n                                   qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1)) for i in range(N)])\n        Jm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jm_spins)\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_collective_down']) * Jm)\n\n    # Collective excitation\n    if 'gamma_collective_up' in rates:\n        Jp_spins = sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, 0.5*qt.sigmap(),\n                                   qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1)) for i in range(N)])\n        Jp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jp_spins)\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_collective_up']) * Jp)\n\n    return c_ops\n```\n\n## Checking Steady State Properties\n\n### Verification\n\n```python\ndef verify_steady_state(rho_ss, H, c_ops, tolerance=1e-6):\n    \"\"\"Verify that rho_ss is indeed a steady state\"\"\"\n\n    L = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n\n    # Compute L[rho]\n    L_rho = L * qt.Qobj(rho_ss.full().flatten()).dag()\n    L_rho = L_rho.reshape(rho_ss.shape)\n\n    # Check if L[rho] ≈ 0\n    norm = L_rho.norm()\n\n    is_steady = norm < tolerance\n\n    result = {\n        'is_steady_state': is_steady,\n        'liouvillian_norm': norm,\n        'trace': rho_ss.tr(),  # should be 1\n        'is_hermitian': np.allclose(rho_ss.full(), rho_ss.full().conj().T),\n        'eigenvalues_positive': np.all(np.linalg.eigvalsh(rho_ss.full()) >= -1e-10)\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n# Usage\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\nprops = verify_steady_state(rho_ss, H, c_ops)\nfor key, val in props.items():\n    print(f\"{key}: {val}\")\n```\n\n### Cavity State Properties\n\n```python\ndef extract_cavity_properties(rho_cavity, n_max):\n    \"\"\"Extract quantum properties from cavity reduced density matrix\"\"\"\n\n    # Number operator\n    n_op = qt.num(n_max)\n\n    # First and second moments\n    photon_mean = qt.expect(n_op, rho_cavity)\n    photon_mean2 = qt.expect(n_op**2, rho_cavity)\n    photon_var = photon_mean2 - photon_mean**2\n\n    # Quadrature operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    x = (a + a.dag()) / np.sqrt(2)  # position\n    p = 1j * (a.dag() - a) / np.sqrt(2)  # momentum\n\n    x_mean = qt.expect(x, rho_cavity)\n    p_mean = qt.expect(p, rho_cavity)\n    x_var = qt.expect(x**2, rho_cavity) - x_mean**2\n    p_var = qt.expect(p**2, rho_cavity) - p_mean**2\n\n    # Purity and entropy\n    purity = float((rho_cavity * rho_cavity).tr())\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n\n    properties = {\n        'photon_mean': photon_mean,\n        'photon_var': photon_var,\n        'x_mean': x_mean,\n        'p_mean': p_mean,\n        'x_variance': x_var,\n        'p_variance': p_var,\n        'purity': purity,\n        'von_neumann_entropy': entropy\n    }\n\n    return properties\n```\n\n## Multiple Cases with Consistent Solvers\n\n### Batch Solving\n\n```python\ndef solve_all_cases(cases_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve steady state for multiple dissipation scenarios\n\n    Args:\n        cases_dict: {case_name: {'H': Hamiltonian, 'c_ops': jump_operators}}\n\n    Returns:\n        {case_name: {'rho_ss': steady_state_density_matrix, ...}}\n    \"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for case_name, case_data in cases_dict.items():\n        print(f\"Solving {case_name}...\")\n\n        H = case_data['H']\n        c_ops = case_data['c_ops']\n\n        # Solve steady state\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=5000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n        # Extract cavity state (subsystem 0)\n        rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n\n        # Store results\n        results[case_name] = {\n            'rho_full': rho_ss,\n            'rho_cavity': rho_cavity,\n            'properties': extract_cavity_properties(rho_cavity, n_max=16)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"  Complete. Photon number: {results[case_name]['properties']['photon_mean']:.4f}\")\n\n    return results\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Issue: Solver Not Converging\n```python\n# Increase solver steps and tolerance\nopts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-10, atol=1e-12)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n# Or try different method\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='iterative')\n```\n\n### Issue: Negative Eigenvalues (Non-Physical State)\n```python\n# Check if problem is properly formulated\n# Verify jump operators are normalized correctly\n# Check Hamiltonian for issues\n\n# Solution: Verify normalization\nfor i, c_op in enumerate(c_ops):\n    print(f\"Jump operator {i} norm: {(c_op.dag()*c_op).tr()}\")\n```\n\n### Issue: Memory Error for Large Systems\n```python\n# Use sparse matrices\n# Consider dimension reduction\n# Use iterative solver instead of direct\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='iterative', options=opts)\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2655,"cache_read_input_tokens":24031,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2655,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a4cfc222-781e-4c6b-b5e3-8e29761b7432","uuid":"32f12bbb-8e24-46b8-8560-d09d47a6e5f6"}

Local excitation/pumping\n    if 'gamma_up' in rates:\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = qt.sigmap()\n            sigma_p_i = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_up']) * sigma_p_i)\n\n    # Collective decay\n    if 'gamma_collective_down' in rates:\n        Jm_spins = sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, 0.5*qt.sigmam(),\n                                   qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1)) for i in range(N)])\n        Jm = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jm_spins)\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_collective_down']) * Jm)\n\n    # Collective excitation\n    if 'gamma_collective_up' in rates:\n        Jp_spins = sum([qt.tensor(qt.qeye(2)**i, 0.5*qt.sigmap(),\n                                   qt.qeye(2)**(N-i-1)) for i in range(N)])\n        Jp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, Jp_spins)\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(rates['gamma_collective_up']) * Jp)\n\n    return c_ops\n```\n\n## Checking Steady State Properties\n\n### Verification\n\n```python\ndef verify_steady_state(rho_ss, H, c_ops, tolerance=1e-6):\n    \"\"\"Verify that rho_ss is indeed a steady state\"\"\"\n\n    L = qt.liouvillian(H, c_ops)\n\n    # Compute L[rho]\n    L_rho = L * qt.Qobj(rho_ss.full().flatten()).dag()\n    L_rho = L_rho.reshape(rho_ss.shape)\n\n    # Check if L[rho] ≈ 0\n    norm = L_rho.norm()\n\n    is_steady = norm < tolerance\n\n    result = {\n        'is_steady_state': is_steady,\n        'liouvillian_norm': norm,\n        'trace': rho_ss.tr(),  # should be 1\n        'is_hermitian': np.allclose(rho_ss.full(), rho_ss.full().conj().T),\n        'eigenvalues_positive': np.all(np.linalg.eigvalsh(rho_ss.full()) >= -1e-10)\n    }\n\n    return result\n\n# Usage\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops)\nprops = verify_steady_state(rho_ss, H, c_ops)\nfor key, val in props.items():\n    print(f\"{key}: {val}\")\n```\n\n### Cavity State Properties\n\n```python\ndef extract_cavity_properties(rho_cavity, n_max):\n    \"\"\"Extract quantum properties from cavity reduced density matrix\"\"\"\n\n    # Number operator\n    n_op = qt.num(n_max)\n\n    # First and second moments\n    photon_mean = qt.expect(n_op, rho_cavity)\n    photon_mean2 = qt.expect(n_op**2, rho_cavity)\n    photon_var = photon_mean2 - photon_mean**2\n\n    # Quadrature operators\n    a = qt.destroy(n_max)\n    x = (a + a.dag()) / np.sqrt(2)  # position\n    p = 1j * (a.dag() - a) / np.sqrt(2)  # momentum\n\n    x_mean = qt.expect(x, rho_cavity)\n    p_mean = qt.expect(p, rho_cavity)\n    x_var = qt.expect(x**2, rho_cavity) - x_mean**2\n    p_var = qt.expect(p**2, rho_cavity) - p_mean**2\n\n    # Purity and entropy\n    purity = float((rho_cavity * rho_cavity).tr())\n    entropy = qt.entropy_vn(rho_cavity)\n\n    properties = {\n        'photon_mean': photon_mean,\n        'photon_var': photon_var,\n        'x_mean': x_mean,\n        'p_mean': p_mean,\n        'x_variance': x_var,\n        'p_variance': p_var,\n        'purity': purity,\n        'von_neumann_entropy': entropy\n    }\n\n    return properties\n```\n\n## Multiple Cases with Consistent Solvers\n\n### Batch Solving\n\n```python\ndef solve_all_cases(cases_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve steady state for multiple dissipation scenarios\n\n    Args:\n        cases_dict: {case_name: {'H': Hamiltonian, 'c_ops': jump_operators}}\n\n    Returns:\n        {case_name: {'rho_ss': steady_state_density_matrix, ...}}\n    \"\"\"\n\n    results = {}\n\n    for case_name, case_data in cases_dict.items():\n        print(f\"Solving {case_name}...\")\n\n        H = case_data['H']\n        c_ops = case_data['c_ops']\n\n        # Solve steady state\n        opts = qt.Options(nsteps=5000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n        rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n        # Extract cavity state (subsystem 0)\n        rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n\n        # Store results\n        results[case_name] = {\n            'rho_full': rho_ss,\n            'rho_cavity': rho_cavity,\n            'properties': extract_cavity_properties(rho_cavity, n_max=16)\n        }\n\n        print(f\"  Complete. Photon number: {results[case_name]['properties']['photon_mean']:.4f}\")\n\n    return results\n```\n\n## Troubleshooting\n\n### Issue: Solver Not Converging\n```python\n# Increase solver steps and tolerance\nopts = qt.Options(nsteps=10000, rtol=1e-10, atol=1e-12)\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n# Or try different method\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='iterative')\n```\n\n### Issue: Negative Eigenvalues (Non-Physical State)\n```python\n# Check if problem is properly formulated\n# Verify jump operators are normalized correctly\n# Check Hamiltonian for issues\n\n# Solution: Verify normalization\nfor i, c_op in enumerate(c_ops):\n    print(f\"Jump operator {i} norm: {(c_op.dag()*c_op).tr()}\")\n```\n\n### Issue: Memory Error for Large Systems\n```python\n# Use sparse matrices\n# Consider dimension reduction\n# Use iterative solver instead of direct\nrho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, method='iterative', options=opts)\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Read CSV with first column as index\n    df = pd.read_csv(filename, index_col=0)\n\n    # Extract coordinates and values\n    pvec = df.index.values\n    xvec = df.columns.astype(float).values\n    W = df.values\n\n    return W, xvec, pvec\n\n# Usage\nW = load_wigner_from_csv('case1.csv')\nW_full, xvec, pvec = load_wigner_with_coordinates('case1.csv')\n```\n\n## Efficient Data Handling\n\n### Memory-Efficient Operations\n\n```python\n# For very large files, use chunked reading\ndef read_wigner_chunked(filename, chunk_size=100):\n    \"\"\"Read large Wigner functions in chunks\"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for chunk in np.genfromtxt(filename, delimiter=',', comments='#',\n                                 skip_header=5, max_rows=chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(chunk)\n    return np.vstack(chunks)\n\n# Compression option (smaller file size, slower to load)\ndef save_wigner_compressed(W, filename):\n    \"\"\"Save as NumPy compressed binary format\"\"\"\n    np.savez_compressed(filename, wigner=W)\n\ndef load_wigner_compressed(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load from compressed NumPy format\"\"\"\n    data = np.load(filename)\n    return data['wigner']\n```\n\n## Batch Operations\n\n### Processing Multiple Cases\n\n```python\ndef process_dicke_cases(cases_dict, output_dir='results'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Complete pipeline: solve cases and save results\n\n    Args:\n        cases_dict: {case_name: {'H': H, 'c_ops': c_ops, 'xvec': xvec, 'pvec': pvec}}\n        output_dir: directory to save results\n    \"\"\"\n    import os\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    file_info = {}\n\n    for case_num, (case_name, case_data) in enumerate(cases_dict.items(), 1):\n        print(f\"Processing {case_name}...\")\n\n        # Save to CSV\n        filename = f'{output_dir}/{case_num}.csv'\n        save_wigner_optimal(W, xvec, pvec, filename, fmt='%.10e')\n\n        # Store file information\n        file_info[case_name] = {\n            'file': filename,\n            'shape': W.shape,\n            'x_range': (xvec[0], xvec[-1]),\n            'p_range': (pvec[0], pvec[-1])\n        }\n\n        print(f\"  Saved to {filename}\")\n\n    return file_info\n\n# Create summary file\ndef create_summary_file(file_info, output_dir='results'):\n    \"\"\"Create a summary of all saved files\"\"\"\n    summary_file = f'{output_dir}/SUMMARY.txt'\n\n    with open(summary_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.write(\"Dicke Model Wigner Function Results Summary\\n\")\n        f.write(\"=\" * 50 + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n        for case_name, info in file_info.items():\n            f.write(f\"Case: {case_name}\\n\")\n            f.write(f\"  File: {info['file']}\\n\")\n            f.write(f\"  Shape: {info['shape']}\\n\")\n            f.write(f\"  x range: {info['x_range']}\\n\")\n            f.write(f\"  p range: {info['p_range']}\\n\")\n            f.write(\"\\n\")\n\n    return summary_file\n```\n\n## Data Validation\n\n### Verifying Saved Data\n\n```python\ndef validate_wigner_csv(filename, expected_shape=(1000, 1000)):\n    \"\"\"Verify saved Wigner function has correct properties\"\"\"\n    W = np.genfromtxt(filename, delimiter=',', comments='#')\n\n    validation = {\n        'shape_correct': W.shape == expected_shape,\n        'shape': W.shape,\n        'has_negative': np.any(W < 0),\n        'min_value': np.min(W),\n        'max_value': np.max(W),\n        'is_finite': np.all(np.isfinite(W)),\n        'file_size_mb': os.path.getsize(filename) / 1e6\n    }\n\n    return validation\n\n# Usage\nval = validate_wigner_csv('case1.csv')\nfor key, val in val.items():\n    print(f\"{key}: {val}\")\n```\n\n## File Organization Examples\n\n### Complete Project Structure\n\n```\nresults/\n├── SUMMARY.txt           # {n_grid} × {n_grid}\")\nprint(f\"  x range: [{x_min}, {x_max}]\")\nprint(f\"  p range: [{p_min}, {p_max}]\")\n\n# Hilbert space dimension\nhilbert_dim = n_max * (2 ** N)\nprint(f\"  Hilbert space dimension: {hilbert_dim}\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# BUILD OPERATORS ON COMPOSITE HILBERT SPACE\n# =============================================================================\n\nprint(f\"\\nBuilding quantum operators...\")\n\n# Identity operators\nI_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\nI_spins = qt.qeye(2 ** N)\n\n# Cavity operators\na = qt.tensor(qt.destroy(n_max), I_spins)\nad = qt.tensor(qt.create(n_max), I_spins)\nn_cav = a.dag() * a\n\n# Collective angular momentum operators for N spins\ndef build_collective_operator(op_type, N, position=None):\n    \"\"\"\n    Build collective spin operators or single-spin operators on composite space.\n\n    op_type: 'Jz', 'Jp', 'Jm', 'sigma_z', 'sigma_p', 'sigma_m'\n    position: qubit index for single-qubit operators (0 to N-1)\n    \"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n\n    if op_type == 'Jz':\n        # Jz = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_z^(i)\n        result = None\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmaz()\n            term = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            result = term if result is None else result + term\n        return result\n\n    elif op_type == 'Jp':\n        # J+ = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_+^(i)\n        result = None\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmap()\n            term = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            result = term if result is None else result + term\n        return result\n\n    elif op_type == 'Jm':\n        # J- = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_-^(i)\n        result = None\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmam()\n            term = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            result = term if result is None else result + term\n        return result\n\n    elif op_type == 'sigma_z' and position is not None:\n        # sigma_z on specific qubit\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[position] = qt.sigmaz()\n        return qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n\n    elif op_type == 'sigma_p' and position is not None:\n        # sigma_+ on specific qubit\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[position] = qt.sigmap()\n        return qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n\n    elif op_type == 'sigma_m' and position is not None:\n        # sigma_- on specific qubit\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[position] = qt.sigmam()\n        return qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown operator type: {op_type}\")\n\n# Build collective operators\nJz = build_collective_operator('Jz', N)\nJp = build_collective_operator('Jp', N)\nJm = build_collective_operator('Jm', N)\n\nprint(\"  Cavity operators: a, a†, n\")\nprint(\"  Collective spin operators: J_z, J_+, J_-\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CONSTRUCT HAMILTONIAN\n# =============================================================================\n\nprint(f\"\\nConstructing Hamiltonian...\")\n\n# H = ω₀ J_z + ω_c a† a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * ad * a + g * (ad + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\nprint(f\"  H = {omega0:.4f} J_z + {omega_c:.4f} a†a + {g:.4f} (a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# DEFINE DISSIPATION CASES\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef create_cavity_decay_ops(gamma):\n    \"\"\"Cavity photon decay\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma) * a]\n\ndef create_local_dephasing_ops(gamma_phi, N):\n    \"\"\"Local dephasing on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_z = build_collective_operator('sigma_z', N, i)\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * sigma_z)\n    return ops\n\ndef create_local_decay_ops(gamma_down, N):\n    \"\"\"Local decay (spontaneous emission) on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_m = build_collective_operator('sigma_m', N, i)\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * sigma_m)\n    return ops\n\ndef create_local_pump_ops(gamma_up, N):\n    \"\"\"Local pumping (excitation) on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_p = build_collective_operator('sigma_p', N, i)\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * sigma_p)\n    return ops\n\ndef create_collective_decay_ops(gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective decay: J_-\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jm]\n\ndef create_collective_pump_ops(gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective pumping: J_+\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jp]\n\n# Define the four dissipation cases\ncases = {\n    'case1': {\n        'name': 'Local dephasing & local pumping',\n        'gamma_phi': 0.01,\n        'gamma_up': 'gamma_collective_down': 0.0\n    },\n    'case4': {\n        'name': 'Local dephasing & local emission & collective emission',\n        'gamma_phi': 0.01,\n        'gamma_up': 0.0,\n {case['name']}\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SOLVE STEADY STATES AND CALCULATE WIGNER FUNCTIONS\n# =============================================================================\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"Solving steady states and calculating Wigner functions\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nresults = {}\nwigner_data = {}\n\nfor case_num, (case_key, case_params) in enumerate(cases.items(), 1):\n    print(f\"\\n[{case_num}/4] Processing {case_key}: {case_params['name']}\")\n\n    # N))\n\n    if case_params['gamma_up'] > 0:\n        c_ops.extend(create_local_pump_ops(case_params['gamma_up'], N))\n\n    if case_params['gamma_down'] > 0:\n c_ops.extend(create_collective_pump_ops(case_params['gamma_collective_up']))\n\n    if case_params['gamma_collective_down'] > 0:\n        c_ops.extend(create_collective_decay_ops(case_params['gamma_collective_down']))\n\n    print(f\"  Jump operators: {len(c_ops)} channels\")\n    print(f\"    - Cavity decay: 1\")\n    print(f\"    - case_params['gamma_down'] > 0 else 0}\")\n    print(f\"    - Collective pumping: {1 if case_params['gamma_collective_up'] > 0 else 0}\")\n    print(f\"    - Collective decay: {1 if case_params['gamma_collective_down'] > 0 else 0}\")\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    print(f\"  Solving steady state...\")\n    opts = qt.Options(nsteps=5000, rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-10)\n    rho_ss = qt.steadystate(H, c_ops, options=opts)\n\n    # Extract cavity reduced density matrix\n    print(f\"  Extracting cavity state...\")\n    rho_cavity = qt.ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n\n    # Calculate Wigner function\n    print(f\"  Calculating Wigner function on {n_grid}×{n_grid} grid...\")\n    W = qt.wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n\n    # Save to CSV file\n    filename = f\"{output_dir}/{case_num}.csv\"\n    print(f\"  Saving to {filename}...\")\n    np.savetxt(filename, W, delimiter=',', fmt='%.10e')\n\n    # Store results\n    results[case_key] = {\n        'rho_full': rho_ss,\n        'rho_cavity': rho_cavity,\n        'W': W,\n        'filename': filename\n    }\n\n    # Calculate cavity state properties for summary\n    n_op = qt.num(n_max)\n    photon_mean = float(qt.expect(n_op, rho_cavity))\n    photon_var = float(qt.expect(n_op**2, rho_cavity) - photon_mean**2)\n    purity = float((rho_cavity * rho_cavity).tr())\n\n    print(f\"  Cavity state properties:\")\n    print(f\"    - Photon number: {photon_mean:.6f} ± {np.sqrt(max(0, photon_var)):.6f}\")\n    print(f\"    - Purity: {purity:.6f}\")\n    print(f\"    - Wigner function range: [{np.min(W):.6e}, {np.max(W):.6e}]\")\n    print(f\"  ✓ Case {case_num} complete\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SUMMARY AND VALIDATION\n# =============================================================================\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"Results Summary\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nprint(f\"\\nSaved files:\")\nfor {case_num}. {filename}\")\n    print(f\"     Shape: {W.shape}, Size: {file_size:.2f} MB\")\n\n# Create summary report\nsummary_file = f\"{output_dir}/SUMMARY.txt\"\nwith open(summary_file, 'w') as     result = None\n        for i in range(N):\n            ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n            ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmaz()\n            term = qt.tensor(I_cavity, qt.tensor(*ops))\n            result = create_local_decay_ops(gamma_down, N):\n    \"\"\"Local decay (spontaneous emission) on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_m = build_collective_operator('sigma_m', ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmaz()\n        term = qt.tensor(*ops)\n        Jz_spin = term if Jz_spin is None else Jz_spin + term\n\n    # J_+ = 0.5 * sum_i sigma_+^(i)\n    Jp_spin = None\n    for i in range(N):\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[i] = 0.5 * qt.sigmap()\n        term = qt.tensor(*ops)\n        Jp_spin = term if Jp_spin is None else Jp_spin + create_cavity_decay_ops(gamma):\n    \"\"\"Cavity photon decay\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma) * a]\n\ndef create_local_dephasing_ops(gamma_phi, N):\n    \"\"\"Local dephasing on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_z_spin = get_spin_operator(N, 'sigma_z', i)\n        sigma_z_composite = qt.tensor(I_cavity, sigma_z_spin)\n I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_m_spin = get_spin_operator(N, 'sigma_m', i)\n        sigma_m_composite = qt.tensor(I_cavity, sigma_m_spin)\n create_collective_pump_ops(gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective pumping: J_+\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jp]\n\n# Define the four dissipation cases\ncases = {\n    'case1': {\n Jm_spin = term if Jm_spin is None else Jm_spin + term\n\n    return Jz_spin, Jp_spin, Jm_spin\n\ndef get_spin_operator(N, op_type, position):\n    \"\"\"Get single-spin operator at specific position in spin space.\"\"\"\n    if op_type == 'sigma_z':\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[position] = qt.sigmaz()\n        return qt.tensor(*ops)\n    elif op_type == 'sigma_p':\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[position] = qt.sigmap()\n        return qt.tensor(*ops)\n    elif op_type == 'sigma_m':\n        ops = [qt.qeye(2)] * N\n        ops[position] = qt.sigmam()\n        return qt.tensor(*ops)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown operator type: {op_type}\")\n\ndef extend_to_composite_space(op_cavity, op_spin):\n    \"\"\"Extend operators to composite cavity+spin space.\"\"\"\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    I_spins = qt.qeye(2 ** N)\n\n    if op_cavity is not None and op_spin is not None:\n        raise ValueError(\"Cannot specify both cavity and spin operators\")\n    elif op_cavity is not None:\n        return qt.tensor(op_cavity, I_spins)\n    elif op_spin is not None:\n        return qt.tensor(I_cavity, op_spin)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(\"Must specify either cavity or spin operator\")\n\n# Build spin operators first\nJz_spin, Jp_spin, Jm_spin = build_spin_operators(N)\n\n# Extend to composite space (and convert to proper dims)\nJz_full = extend_to_composite_space(None, Jz_spin)\nJp_full = extend_to_composite_space(None, Jp_spin)\nJm_full = extend_to_composite_space(None, Jm_spin)\n\n# Convert to proper composite Hilbert space dimensions\n# This flattens [16, 2, 2, 2, 2] to [16, 16] for consistency\nJz = qt.Qobj(Jz_full.full(), dims=[[n_max, 2**N], [n_max, 2**N]])\nJp = qt.Qobj(Jp_full.full(), dims=[[n_max, 2**N], [n_max, 2**N]])\nJm = qt.Qobj(Jm_full.full(), dims=[[n_max, 2**N], [n_max, 2**N]])\n\nprint(\"  Cavity operators: a, a†, n\")\nprint(\"  Collective spin operators: J_z, J_+, J_-\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CONSTRUCT HAMILTONIAN\n# =============================================================================\n\nprint(f\"\\nConstructing Hamiltonian...\")\n\n# H = ω₀ J_z + ω_c a† a + g(a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\nH = omega0 * Jz + omega_c * ad * a + g * (ad + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\nprint(f\"  H = {omega0:.4f} J_z + {omega_c:.4f} a†a + {g:.4f} (a† + a)(J₊ + J₋)\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# DEFINE DISSIPATION CASES\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef create_cavity_decay_ops(gamma):\n    \"\"\"Cavity photon decay\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma) * a]\n\ndef create_local_dephasing_ops(gamma_phi, N):\n    \"\"\"Local dephasing on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_z_spin = get_spin_operator(N, 'sigma_z', i)\n        sigma_z_temp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, sigma_z_spin)\n        sigma_z = qt.Qobj(sigma_z_temp.full(), dims=[[n_max, 2**N], [n_max, 2**N]])\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * sigma_z)\n    return ops\n\ndef create_local_decay_ops(gamma_down, N):\n    \"\"\"Local decay (spontaneous emission) on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_m_spin = get_spin_operator(N, 'sigma_m', i)\n        sigma_m_temp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, sigma_m_spin)\n        sigma_m = qt.Qobj(sigma_m_temp.full(), dims=[[n_max, 2**N], [n_max, 2**N]])\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * sigma_m)\n    return ops\n\ndef create_local_pump_ops(gamma_up, N):\n    \"\"\"Local pumping (excitation) on each qubit\"\"\"\n    ops = []\n    I_cavity = qt.qeye(n_max)\n    for i in range(N):\n        sigma_p_spin = get_spin_operator(N, 'sigma_p', i)\n        sigma_p_temp = qt.tensor(I_cavity, sigma_p_spin)\n        sigma_p = qt.Qobj(sigma_p_temp.full(), dims=[[n_max, 2**N], [n_max, 2**N]])\n        ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * sigma_p)\n    return ops\n\ndef create_collective_decay_ops(gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective decay: J_-\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jm]\n\ndef create_collective_pump_ops(gamma_collective):\n    \"\"\"Collective pumping: J_+\"\"\"\n    return [np.sqrt(gamma_collective) * Jp]\n\n# Define the four dissipation cases\ncases = {\n    'case1': {\n        'name': 'Local dephasing & local pumping',\n        'gamma_phi': 0.01,\n        'gamma_up': 0.1,\n        'gamma_down': 0.0,\n        'gamma_collective_up': 0.0,\n        'gamma_collective_down': 0.0\n    },\n    'case2': {\n        'name': 'Local dephasing & local emission',\n        'gamma_phi': 0.01,\n        'gamma_up': 0.0,\n        'gamma_down': 0.1,\n        'gamma_collective_up': 0.0,\n        'gamma_collective_down': 0.0\n    },\n    'case3': {\n        'name': 'Local dephasing & local emission & collective pumping',\n        'gamma_phi': 0.01,\n        'gamma_up': 0.0,\n        'gamma_down': 0.1,\n        'gamma_collective_up': 0.1,\n        'gamma_collective_down': 0.0\n    },\n    'case4': {\n        'name': 'Local dephasing & local emission & }\n}\n\nprint(f\"\\nDissipation Cases:\")\nfor key, case in cases.items():\n    print(f\"  {key}: {case['name']}\")\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SOLVE STEADY STATES AND CALCULATE WIGNER FUNCTIONS\n# =============================================================================\n\nprint(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\nprint(\"Solving steady states and calculating Wigner functions\")\nprint(\"=\" * 70)\n\nresults = {}\nwigner_data = {}\n\nfor case_num, (case_key, case_params) in enumerate(cases.items(), 1):\n    print(f\"\\n[{case_num}/4] Processing {case_key}: {case_params['name']}\")\n\n    # case_params['gamma_down'] > 0 else 0}\")\n    print(f\"    - Collective pumping: {1 if case_params['gamma_collective_up'] > 0 else 0}\")\n    print(f\"    - Collective decay: {1 if {case_num}. {filename}\")\n    print(f\"     Shape: {W.shape}, Size: {file_size:.2f} MB\")\n\n# Create summary report\nsummary_file = f\"{output_dir}/SUMMARY.txt\"\nwith open(summary_file, 'w') as
